### 5

Provenance: outside-evidence row. Actor: QA operator. Surface: docs / operational evidence. Steps and expected observable: Recount the pre-removal Spec 0203 report from a1fc8402^ and compare the independent published Hash1 summary definition.

```yaml
inputs:
  - kind: repository_path
    ref: docs/adr/0210-an-evidence-snapshot-records-one-digest-per-declared-input.md
  - kind: commit_range
    ref: a1fc8402^
  - kind: live_service
    ref: https://pkg.go.dev/golang.org/x/mod/sumdb/dirhash#Hash1
```

Observation: Historical Git measurement asserted exactly 157,212 report lines, 78,254 sha256 lines, 156,696 snapshot-block lines, 11 rows and 77 inputs (7 per row). The new form computes 1 + 3 × 11 + 77 = 111 lines. [Historical recount](evidence/2026-10-01-gate/row-5-history.json). Independent `git ls-tree -r --name-only <recorded-head> -- go.mod` plus `git show <recorded-head>:go.mod` and Python hashlib confirms the legacy content digest and computes its one-input summary digest, without production Go helpers: [digest evidence](evidence/2026-10-01-gate/row-5-digest.json). Fresh published [Go Hash1 documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/golang.org/x/mod/sumdb/dirhash#Hash1) confirms sorted filename order, hexadecimal content SHA-256, two spaces, filename and newline, with newline names rejected; ADR-0210 differs only in final digest encoding. [Source observation](evidence/2026-10-01-gate/row-5-published-source.md). Both sources predate or originate outside this Spec; no invented fixture substitutes for them.

## Execution commands

All focused commands use `GOCACHE=/private/tmp/roundfix-0210-qa-cache`, with output captured outside the repository until every concurrent test completes, then copied to the linked logs. These are QA behavior checks, not another repository Verification.

```sh
rtk proxy go test -count=1 -v -run '^(TestEvidenceRecordWritesOneLinePerInputWhateverItMatches|TestEvidenceInputDigestFollowsTheSortedSummary|TestCarryComparesTheRecordedDigestPerInput|TestCarryNamesTheMovedRefNotItsFiles|TestCarriableAcceptsARecordedDigestAgainstTheCurrentFiles|TestEvidenceRecordSnapshotsEveryQualifyingPassRow)$' ./internal/speccheck
rtk proxy go test -count=1 -v -run '^(TestCarriable|TestMechanicalStageCarriable.*|TestRowCarry.*|TestCarryReadsAPerFileSnapshotAsItsDigest|TestEvidenceRecord.*)$' ./internal/speccheck
rtk proxy go test -count=1 -v -run '^(TestTwoGatePassesCarryAnUnmovedRowIntoTheCommittedReport|TestTwoGatePassesReRunARowWhoseInputMoved|TestQAGateCarriesARowFromAnUnintegratedFailedPass|TestQAGate.*Snapshot.*|TestPriorQAPass.*)$' ./internal/daemon
```

Each exited 0 on the corrected capture run: speccheck 4.946s, speccheck 9.234s, daemon 6.491s. The named tests were present and executed, with top-level PASS lines and no empty test selection. Scratch Git repositories and Daemon fixtures confirm committed/reloaded reports, providing persistence checks without a live ACP Runtime. No API or interactive frontend is declared here.

## Findings

No product finding. Evidence-capture correction: the first commands exited 1 after their tests passed because writing logs into the checkout violated suiteguard’s unchanged-repository boundary. The three initial logs remain as `row-2-capture-error.log`, `row-3-capture-error.log` and `row-4-capture-error.log` under the evidence directory. Redirecting output to /private/tmp corrected the cause; all reruns passed with the guard intact. No code, test, fixture or verification configuration changed.
